2026.11.26 晚报

覆盖窗口：2026-11-26T06:00:00+08:00 → 2026-11-26T21:30:00+08:00 · 更新时间：2026.11.26 21:57 北京时间 · 281 条

窗口：2026-11-26 06:00 至 2026-11-26 21:30（北京时间，UTC+8）｜监控名单 134 个账号｜所有时间均为北京时间。

Executive Summary｜核心结论

本期窗口为北京时间 2026-11-26 06:00 至 21:30（UTC 2026-11-25T22:00:00Z 含，至 2026-11-26T13:30:00Z 不含），是早报与晚报之间的 15.5 小时区间，不是滚动 24 小时，不与 11-26 早报重叠。134 个监控账号全部分页至末页；本区间是美股感恩节休市日（11 月 26 日）前后，亚洲盘方面，名单外帖子称日股在 11 月 25 日 AI/半导体暴跌后于 26 日反弹；监控名单内 70 个账号无原创/回复，整体发帖量偏低。以下【分析】是本摘要的判断；“据称/作者认为/报道称”是发帖人或媒体的说法，未经独立核实；名单外关键词条目（k 编号）另有标注。

AI 信贷｜重新定价已从股权蔓延到信用：贷款方给“合同”定价、给借款人打折，出口管制让中国相关融资直接冻结

@nodiligence（n=552，19:57）汇总 11 月 25 日的信用面：CoreWeave CDS 创 1,314bp 纪录，同日银行为其安排约 7% 利率、由 $META 合同支撑的 45 亿美元贷款（其自身债券收益率约 18%）；S&P 对 $NVDA 发出降级警告，并指 NVDA 对 OpenAI 俄亥俄园区的 1,050 亿美元担保在公开条款中无追加抵押条款；亚洲贷款方对 77.5 亿美元数据中心交易追加芯片最终用户审查。@aleabitoreddit（n=12，06:17）称 CRWV 这笔 45 亿美元贷款初步定价 7%–7.5%、认购已与发行规模相当。名单外 k14 称 NVDA 盘中一度 -9.2%、收 -3.9%，信用没有跟随反弹；k3 称 SoftBank 5 年 CDS 升至 516bp、S&P 警告其 LTV 已超 30%、逼近 35% 的降级考虑门槛。

出口管制的融资传导：@dnystedt（n=158，10:14）引 Bloomberg 称 11 月 16 日远程访问新规后，贷款方开始停止与字节跳动、阿里巴巴、腾讯相关的数据中心贷款，包括接近完成的交易；CNBC 称大量原本供中国租户使用的 Nvidia GPU 被挂牌出售；DigiTimes 称 BIS 扩大 AI 服务器客户审查，已获批订单也须重审。@jasonschips（n=47）称 Citi 与渣打收紧对东南亚数据中心客户的审查。

【分析】信用市场在做“交易对手分层”：有 hyperscaler 合同背书的资产仍能以 ~7% 融资，而 neocloud 自身信用、SoftBank 这类杠杆持有人和中国相关项目被系统性折价。对 $NVDA 而言，风险从需求转向“客户能否融到钱”和或有担保敞口；被挂牌出售的中国用途 GPU 是二手/现货价格的潜在压力源。以上 CDS、担保与贷款数字均为 X 上转述，未独立核实。

存储｜第三方开始下修 2027 年价格斜率，但总量预测仍在上调：DRAM/HBM 与 NAND 分化，LTA 的约束力受质疑

下修：@sssjeffpu（n=478，17:13）称 2027 年 1Q–4Q DRAM 合约价预测从环比 +10%/+7%/+5%/+3% 下修为 +6%/+3%/0%/0%，原因是中国 CSP 服务器 DRAM 订单疲软（约占总需求 1%）与消费级规格下调，但 2026–28E 供给缺口仍为 -5%/-5%/-4%，作者仍对 $MU、$SKHY 持建设性看法、对 DDR4（南亚科、华邦）不利。@dnystedt（n=164）指出 TrendForce 虽“下调”，2027 年 DRAM 市场仍为 1.15 万亿美元（+69%）、NAND 4,193 亿美元（+29%），均高于 5 月版本。

质疑合同质量：@jukan05（n=329，13:57）引韩媒称 SK hynix 合同负债一年来停在约 5,000 亿韩元，Samsung 二季度合同负债环比 +8.5 万亿韩元后三季度仅 +5,500 亿韩元，业内推测 Samsung 以较低预付款换取更高价格上限——预付款越低，供需逆转时客户越容易违约。名单外 k9 则称美光 1,340 亿美元 RPO 中仅约四分之一在 12 个月内确认，多数战略协议设价格上下限。名单外 k6 称三星证券测算当前估值已隐含 Samsung 46%、SK hynix 36% 的存储价格跌幅。

偏多/结构性：@dnystedt（n=160）称 SanDisk 预测 2030 年 AI 数据中心 NAND 达 1.2ZB 且“没有下行风险”；n=378 称 Nvidia 与 Kioxia 在一个 GB200 NVL72 机柜挂载约 1.1PB 闪存作为 GPU 扩展内存（概念验证，无性能数据），Kioxia 当日大涨；@BenBajarin（n=364）认为部分存储 SKU 已呈现“类逻辑芯片”定价动态。风险项：@jukan05（n=229）称美光可能搁置 1.6 万亿新台币（约 485 亿美元）台湾投资（未经证实）；名单外 k1 称 USTR Section 301 报告点名 Samsung/SK hynix 合计控制 DRAM 70%+，但存储暂豁免 25% 关税；名单外 k8 称 Samsung/SK hynix 已与长江存储签 Xtacking 交叉授权。

【分析】市场分歧不在 2027 年量，而在 2027 下半年之后的价格斜率与合同可执行性：如果 LTA 预付款偏低，“锁量锁价”对下行的保护比管理层宣称的弱，这是 $SKHY/Samsung 估值折价的合理来源；DRAM/HBM 与 NAND 的分化继续，Kioxia–Nvidia 的 SSD 扩展内存若落地，是 NAND 需求端少见的新增量叙事。

需求｜capex 与 token 指标仍在加速，股价与基本面背离

@techfund1（n=393，15:11）引 JPMorgan 美国团队预测 hyperscaler 合计 capex 2027 年 +87%、2028 年 +19%；n=386 引 UBS 渠道调研称 Snowflake 四家合作伙伴均看到客户支出增长“异常强劲、在很多情况下加速”。@TradexWhisperer（n=266）称 OpenRouter token 量一年增长 35 倍、三个月 3.5 倍，agentic 负载约占 80%。@AravSrinivas（n=284，12:58）称 Gemini 4 Neon 在 Perplexity 上（无默认路由）用量逐日翻倍、单日超过 1,000 亿 token。@BenBajarin（n=236）称当前所有 GW 级需求覆盖不到全球企业的 20%，“非常非常非常早期”。

价格面：名单外 k18/k20 列出的回撤（SNDK 约 -75%、CRWV 约 -78%、NVDA 36 天约 -49%、SMH 110 天 -41%）与 k10 称 $MU 仅 3.4 倍 FY27 一致预期 EPS，均为名单外作者说法。@jukan05（n=295）称韩国考虑将股市稳定基金从 10.7 万亿韩元扩大到 20 万亿韩元以上；@dnystedt（n=387）称韩国央行把 2027 年增长预测从 2.9% 上调至 4.1%，芯片公司今年营业利润接近 700 万亿韩元。

【分析】本区间的一手需求数据（capex 预测、token 量、渠道调研）都在加速，而价格端已按“融资断裂 + 2028 周期见顶”定价。预期差的核心在于：需求方的现金流（hyperscaler）与承担融资风险的一方（neocloud、SoftBank、存储厂预付款）是不同的主体——基本面强不等于整条链条的信用都安全。

模型竞争｜OpenAI 实质涨价、Google Neon 用量爆发、Opus Next 速度领先；中国实验室受蒸馏与算力双重约束

@thsottiaux（n=189，10:50）称 GPT-6.2 发布时的计费错误已修复，用户触达 Astra 限额会快 40%、Sol 快 25%（上周超额不收费，并给一次额度重置），@kimmonismus（n=340、498）等用户称“几乎没法用”、考虑退订。SemiAnalysis GDPx（n=84、88，08:09）：GPT-6.2 Astra 以 19.3% 登顶，但软件工程仅 13.3%（Fable 5.2 为 24.4%）；OpenAI 占据成本-性能前沿，Sol 以每任务 $9.22 得分 11.5%，与 Opus 5.5 相差不到半个百分点。@chetaslua（n=448）称 Opus Next 峰值 291 tok/s、TTFT 较 5.5 快一倍（个人测试，未发布）。@gdb（n=313）称 OpenAI Private Inference 进入测试。

中国：@jukan05（n=495，17:41）与 @kimmonismus（n=517）引 Reuters 称智谱 3 万亿参数 GLM-5.5 可能推迟到 2027 年一季度或更晚，月之暗面估计若失去海外租用算力，大版本间隔将从 6 个月拉长到 12 个月以上、Kimi K4 或推迟到 2027 年年中，并称月之暗面已改从 Google/Meta 模型蒸馏；@zephyr_z9（n=568）称智谱 IR 已否认此前相关报道。@teortaxesTex（n=437）称 DeepSeek 当前一代在硬科学基准上明显落后。

【分析】OpenAI 的“修复计费”本质上是对重度用户的有效涨价，短期利好单位经济、但在 Opus Next 与 Neon 同时发力时有流失风险；Google 借 Neon + 额度重置在 agent/编程订阅上抢用量。中国实验室的发布节奏若如 Reuters 所述被拉长，海外开源竞争压力下降，对美国前沿实验室定价权是边际利好。Opus Next 数据为个人测试，Reuters 报道已被智谱部分否认，均需跟踪。

定制芯片与供应链｜TPU/Trainium perf/$ 进入第三方基准，Nvidia CoWoS 份额下滑；电力链仍有订单

@SemiAnalysis_（n=441，16:22）称 Trainium 与 TPU 已登上 InferenceX/AgentX，每美元性能为 NVIDIA GPU 的 1.2–2.5 倍；@jasonschips（n=47）称 Trainium3 UltraServer 在 agentic 推理基准上 perf/$ 比 GB300 NVL72 高 2.28 倍（特定负载）。@dnystedt（n=165）引投行称 Nvidia 2027 年 CoWoS 需求 +57% 至 122 万片晶圆，但在台积电 CoWoS 中的占比从 53.4% 降至 45.6%，份额流向 Google TPU 与云端 ASIC。@tphuang（n=4）与 @dnystedt（n=381）分别提到华为 4096-DQ HBM 与 Samsung 500+ 层 NAND 将在 ISSCC 2027 发表。

电力：@stop_loss_stop（n=44，07:02）列出 Bloom Energy 相关韩国 5 家供应商合同合计约 1.49 万亿韩元；名单外 k16 称广达向 Bloom 采购两套系统共约 3.67 亿美元，k17 称 Morgan Stanley 给 $BE 335 美元目标价。@Omercheema（n=455）认为 AI 在 EDA 栈内提升生产力，但不改写 Synopsys/Cadence 双寡头格局。

【分析】ASIC 的 perf/$ 优势首次在第三方推理基准上被系统性呈现，叠加 CoWoS 份额迁移，指向 $NVDA 推理份额的结构性稀释而非总量下降；受益方是 $GOOGL、$AMZN 与 $AVGO 的定制芯片链。电力侧（$BE）订单持续，但与 AI 信用收紧存在时间差风险。

Highlights / Top 10｜按投资重要性排序

#2 ★@dnystedt · 2026-11-26 10:14 北京时间

要点：据 Bloomberg 报道，11 月 16 日限制中国访问顶级 Nvidia 芯片的远程访问规则出台后，数十亿美元的数据中心交易陷入停滞，贷款方已开始停止发放与中国公司字节跳动、阿里巴巴和腾讯相关的贷款——甚至包括已接近完成的交易。
据 CNBC 报道，新规还导致异常大量的 Nvidia GPU 被挂牌出售，因为它们原本是要用于中国公司租用算力的数据中心。
与此同时，据 DigiTimes 报道，美国商务部（BIS）一项扩大 AI 服务器客户审查的新规意味着更严格的管控和官僚程序。即使是已获批的订单也必须重新审查，流程中还增加了更多文书工作。（附三个来源链接）

为何重要：11 月 16 日远程访问新规后，贷款方停止与字节/阿里/腾讯相关的数据中心贷款、中国用途 GPU 被挂牌出售；出口管制已直接传导到融资与 GPU 二手供给。

#4 @sssjeffpu · 2026-11-26 17:13 北京时间

要点：存储：DRAM 价格温和下修
• 据领先的存储研究机构 @trendforce，DRAM 价格进入 2027 年可能趋缓。
我们的看法 ⬇️
• 2027 年 1Q/2Q/3Q/4Q 的 DRAM 合约价展望下修 → 现为环比 +6%/+3%/0%/0% → 此前为 +10%/+7%/+5%/+3%
• GPU 短缺导致中国 CSP 的服务器 DRAM 订单疲软（约占 DRAM 总需求的 1%，美国 CSP 可以消化）
• 消费级 DRAM 疲软 + 规格下调 + DDR4 承压
• 供需仍将良好：2026/27/28E 的供给缺口为 -5%/-5%/-4%，由 HBM 挤占晶圆产能驱动 → 若疲软持续，可能减产（以支撑 LTA 底价）→ DRAM 供需平衡好于 NAND
影响
• 由于下修幅度有限，仍对 Mi……（全文见下方分作者部分）

为何重要：2027 年 DRAM 合约价季度斜率从 +10/+7/+5/+3% 下修为 +6/+3/0/0%，但供给缺口仍为 -5%；量化了“温和下修”的幅度，对 $MU/$SKHY 盈利斜率关键。

#5 @jukan05 · 2026-11-26 13:57 北京时间

要点：Samsung Electronics 和 SK hynix 声称其长期协议（LTA）现在比以前更具约束力，但韩国媒体报道称，其账面上记录的预付款难以支撑这一说法。
根据 SK hynix 2026 年三季报，合同负债（即收到客户预付款后交付产品的义务）在过去一年里几乎一直停留在约 5,000 亿韩元附近。公司解释称，由于交易通过保证金等多种方式进行，仅凭合同负债或预付款难以判断长期协议。然而，托管（escrow）、担保和抵押都是在合同被违约时只能通过既定程序才能收回资金的安排。如果公司真的加强了协议的约束力，那么选择这些方式而不是直接收取预付款，应该另有原因。一位会计师还指出，托管并非常见安排，因为公司只要直接收钱并加以运用，至少就能赚到利息，没有理由把钱存放在第三方。
Samsung Electronics……（全文见下方分作者部分）

为何重要：韩媒用合同负债数据质疑 Samsung/SK hynix LTA 的约束力：Samsung 三季度预付款仅 +5,500 亿韩元；关系到存储下行时合同对价格的保护强度。

原帖：https://x.com/jukan05/status/2125831326389694484 ｜ 跳至完整条目

#6 ★@dnystedt · 2026-11-26 10:24 北京时间

要点：据媒体援引投行分析师报道，Nvidia 对 CoWoS 先进封装的需求将在 2027 年继续上升，预计增长 57% 至 122 万片晶圆，但随着竞争对手拿走更多产能，其在台积电 CoWoS 产能中的占比将下降。Nvidia 的占比将从今年的 53.4% 降至 45.6%，而 Google TPU 和其他云端 ASIC 将获得更多产能。$NVDA $TSM $GOOGL $AMZN $AVGO #semiconductors

为何重要：Nvidia 2027 年 CoWoS 需求 +57% 至 122 万片，但在台积电 CoWoS 占比从 53.4% 降至 45.6%；ASIC 份额迁移的供应链证据。

#8 @thsottiaux · 2026-11-26 10:50 北京时间

要点：我们在 GPT-6.2 发布时犯了一个计费错误，导致大多数用户的 GPT-6.2 Astra 和 Sol 用量被错误地打了折。
我们现在已经修复，所以同样的工作会消耗你套餐中更多的用量额度。与上周相比，你在 GPT-6.2 Astra 上触达限额的速度会快 40%，在 Sol 上快 25%。与 6 Astra 和 6.1 Sol 相比，你应该仍会看到这些模型更耐用。其他模型不受影响，过去的用量差额我们不会向你收费。
作为道歉的小小心意，我们会为所有 Go、Plus、Pro 和 Business 用户增加一次 banked reset（储存的额度重置）。

为何重要：OpenAI 修复 GPT-6.2 计费：Astra 触限快 40%、Sol 快 25%；对重度用户的实质涨价，影响订阅单位经济与用户流失。

DAY 69：$CRWV $NVDA $META $BTBT $WYFI $CLSK $APLD $GS
我每天都在更新这张 AI/算力/宏观地图：
—— AI 信贷在 11 月 25 日被重新定价，而贷款背后的"兜底方"与借款人同时变弱
—— CoreWeave 的 CDS 创下 1,314bp 的纪录，同一天它的银行为其安排了一笔约 7% 利率、由 Meta 合同支撑的 45 亿美元贷款。它自己的债券收益率约 18%。贷款方是在给 Meta 的合同定价，而把 CoreWeave 本身视为风险
—— NVIDIA 收到了 S&P 的降级警告。它为 OpenAI 俄亥俄园区提供的 1050 亿美元担保在公开条款中没有追加抵押条款，但其背后的贷款方是按 AA 级兜底来定价的
—— Bit Digital 卖出 5000 万美元 ETH，把其通往 WhiteFiber 的过桥融资背后的 Galaxy 贷款减半。WhiteFiber 的 3 亿美元贷款已签署，但截至 11 月 10 日尚未放款，所以母公司充当了缓冲垫
—— CleanSpark 在场地电力转给 AI 租户后，将旧矿机减记至账面价值的 39%。电力变得更值钱，机器则贬值了
—— 亚洲贷款方对 77.5 亿美元的数据中心交易追加了关于芯片最终使用者的审查。如果服务不被允许，再强的担保也救不了交易（附图）

-- AI credit got repriced on 25 november, and the backups behind the loans got weaker at the same time as the borrowers
-- coreweave's CDS hit a record 1,314bp the same day its banks lined up a $4.5bn meta-backed loan at about 7%. its own bonds yield about 18%. lenders are pricing meta's contract and treating coreweave itself as the risk
-- NVIDIA got a downgrade warning from S&P. its $105bn guarantee for openAI's ohio campus has no collateral call in its public terms, but the lenders behind it priced an AA backstop
-- bit digital sold $50m of ETH to halve the galaxy loan behind its bridge to whitefiber. whitefiber's $300m loan is signed but hadn't funded by 10 november, so the parent is the shock absorber
-- cleanspark marked its old mining gear to 39% of book once the site's power went to an AI tenant. the power got more valuable and the machines lost value

当前形势与 2025 年 4 月的相似之处：
进入周末前：指数在周四和周五下跌 10%，今年也一样。
周一：2025 年盘前一度跌 5%，随后大幅反弹收红；这次是高开高收。
周二：两次都深度收跌、收在最低点，并且都有关税话题延续到周三。
周三：两次都是深度低开后反转。
情绪：两次 VIX 都触及 40，CPC（看跌/看涨比）都超过 1.2。
这次我对判断见底没那么有信心，因为我们处于周期更后段，但确实有一些有意思的信号（见底也不是我的基准情形）。

原文：Similarities between the current setup and April 2025:

Leading into the weekend: Indices dropped 10% over Thursday and Friday. Same thing happened this year.

Monday: In 2025, we were down 5% in pre-market before a big rally to close green. This time, it was a green open and a green close.

Tuesday: Deep red both times, closing at the lows, with tariff talk leading into Wednesday.

Wednesday: Deep red open followed by a reversal both times.

Sentiment: VIX hit 40 and CPC went over 1.2 in both cases.

I feel less confident calling a bottom this time since we’re later in the cycle, but there are definitely some interesting signs (bottom not my base case either)

据 Bloomberg 报道，11 月 16 日限制中国访问顶级 Nvidia 芯片的远程访问规则出台后，数十亿美元的数据中心交易陷入停滞，贷款方已开始停止发放与中国公司字节跳动、阿里巴巴和腾讯相关的贷款——甚至包括已接近完成的交易。
据 CNBC 报道，新规还导致异常大量的 Nvidia GPU 被挂牌出售，因为它们原本是要用于中国公司租用算力的数据中心。
与此同时，据 DigiTimes 报道，美国商务部（BIS）一项扩大 AI 服务器客户审查的新规意味着更严格的管控和官僚程序。即使是已获批的订单也必须重新审查，流程中还增加了更多文书工作。（附三个来源链接）

原文：Billions in data center deals have ground to a halt after the Nov. 16 remote access rule restricting China access to the top Nvidia chips, Bloomberg reports, as lenders have begun to stop making loans tied to China firms ByteDance, Alibaba and Tencent – even deals nearing completion.

The new rule has also seen an unusually large number of Nvidia GPUs put up for sale because they were originally destined for data centers which China firms would rent access, CNBC reports.

Meanwhile, a new US Commerce Dept. (BIS) rule widening customer checks on AI servers means more strict control and bureaucracy, DigiTimes reports. Even orders that were already approved will have to be reexamined, and more paperwork has been added to the process.

TrendForce 下调了 2027 年 DRAM 和 NAND Flash 存储芯片市场预测，但此前的数字似乎并未广泛公布。与其 5 月发布的版本相比，两项预测其实都上调了。
2027 年 DRAM 市场下调至 1.15 万亿美元，较 2026 年的 6,835 亿美元同比增长 69%
2027 年 NAND 市场下调至 4,193 亿美元，较 2026 年的 3,244 亿美元增长 29%
而 5 月广泛公布的 2027 年预测是：
DRAM 9,033 亿美元，较 2026 年的 6,187 亿美元增长 46%
NAND 3,794 亿美元，较 2026 年的 2,706 亿美元增长 40.2%
（TrendForce 11 月 25 日与 5 月 29 日来源链接）

原文：TrendForce cut its 2027 DRAM and NAND Flash memory chip market forecasts, but the prior figures appear not to have been widely published. Compared to its May release, it raised both forecasts.
2027 DRAM market cut to US$1.15 trillion, up 69% year-on year, from $683.5 billion in 2026
2027 NAND market cut to US$419.3 billion, up 29% from $324.4 billion in 2026

But the May forecasts for 2027, widely published, were:
DRAM US$903.3 billion, up 46% from $618.7B in 2026

NAND US$379.4 billion, up 40.2% from $270.6 billion in 2026

2026-11-26 14:53 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@dnystedt ｜ 原帖链接

Nvidia 和日本的 Kioxia 展示了 NAND 闪存可以充当额外的 GPU 内存。两家公司表示，在一个测试机柜中，Nvidia 的 SCADA 软件以 512 字节的小块，从 144 块 Kioxia XD8 7.68TB NVMe 固态硬盘读取数据，相当于在一个 Nvidia GB200 NVL72 机柜上挂载约 1.1PB 闪存。这只是概念验证，没有给出速度、延迟或功耗结果。闪存比 HBM 慢得多，所以它是对 HBM 的补充而非替代，承担不需要最高速度的工作，比如超大向量数据库。如果可行，未来的 AI 服务器可能会装载多得多的 NAND。该结果消息推动 Kioxia 股价在东京上涨。$NVDA #Kioxia #semiconductors

原文：Nvidia and Japan's Kioxia showed NAND flash can act as extra GPU memory. In a test rack, Nvidia's SCADA software read data in small 512-byte pieces from 144 Kioxia XD8 7.68TB NVMe solid-state drives (SSDs), about 1.1PB of flash on one Nvidia GB200 NVL72 rack, the companies said. It's a proof of concept, with no speed, latency or power results given. Flash is far slower than HBM, so it adds to HBM rather than replacing it, taking on jobs that don't need top speeds, like huge vector databases. If it works, future AI servers could hold a lot more NAND. News of the results sent Kioxia shares higher in Tokyo. $NVDA #Kioxia #semiconductors https://t.co/xNlESC15Mo

2026-11-26 10:25 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@dnystedt ｜ 原帖链接

据 Semafor 报道，ASML CEO Christophe Fouquet 表示，出口管制应保护最先进的技术，但不应完全切断中国。向中国出售落后一代的设备能让它保持依赖，而不是逼它"加倍甚至三倍投入"自研。
"你需要（出口管制）来保护非常先进的技术，但使用出口管制的方式也必须避免让一个国家彻底受挫，从而促使它基本上加倍或三倍投入研发，"Fouquet 在 Semafor 的 Future Proof 上说。
"近 30 年来，我们在中国一直保持极高的市场份额——极高意味着接近 100%——这意味着他们接受了依赖我们的技术，前提是他们能获得这些技术，"他说。

原文：ASML CEO Christophe Fouquet said export controls should protect the most advanced technology without completely cutting China off, Semafor reports. Selling China equipment that is a generation behind keeps it dependent instead of pushing it to "double down or triple down" on building its own, he said.

“You need [export controls] to protect very advanced technology, but you also have to use export control in such a way that you don’t completely frustrate a country and invite that country to basically double down or triple down on development,” Fouquet said on Semafor’s Future Proof.

“For almost 30 years, we have been able to keep an extremely high market share in China — very high means close to 100% — which means that they have accepted to rely on our technology with the idea that they had access to it,” he said.

2026-11-26 13:57 北京时间 ｜ Source: @jukan05 ｜ 原帖链接

Samsung Electronics 和 SK hynix 声称其长期协议（LTA）现在比以前更具约束力，但韩国媒体报道称，其账面上记录的预付款难以支撑这一说法。
根据 SK hynix 2026 年三季报，合同负债（即收到客户预付款后交付产品的义务）在过去一年里几乎一直停留在约 5,000 亿韩元附近。公司解释称，由于交易通过保证金等多种方式进行，仅凭合同负债或预付款难以判断长期协议。然而，托管（escrow）、担保和抵押都是在合同被违约时只能通过既定程序才能收回资金的安排。如果公司真的加强了协议的约束力，那么选择这些方式而不是直接收取预付款，应该另有原因。一位会计师还指出，托管并非常见安排，因为公司只要直接收钱并加以运用，至少就能赚到利息，没有理由把钱存放在第三方。
Samsung Electronics 的情况不同。其合同负债多年来一直在一定区间内，但随着与大客户签订长期协议，今年二季度环比增加约 8.5 万亿韩元至 23.6 万亿韩元。公司曾表示随着更多合同签订，预付款会进一步增加。然而在三季度，与预付款相关的合同负债仅增加约 5,500 亿韩元。业内认为，Samsung 可能在追加合同中减少了预付款金额，以换取提高半导体价格上限。如果这种解读正确，就与公司"约束力充分的 LTA"的说法不符。因为预付款越低，一旦存储供需平衡逆转，客户就越容易退出合同。

原文：Samsung Electronics and SK hynix say their long term agreements (LTAs) are now more binding than before, but Korean media reported that the advance payments recorded on their books make that claim hard to support.

According to SK hynix's 2026 third quarter report, contract liabilities, which represent the obligation to deliver products after receiving payment from customers in advance, have barely moved from around KRW 500 billion over the past year. The company explained that because transactions are conducted through various methods such as deposits, it is difficult to judge long term agreements based on contract liabilities or advance payments alone. However, escrow, guarantees, and collateral are arrangements where money can only be recovered through set procedures if a contract is broken. If the company had truly strengthened the binding force of its agreements, there should be some other reason for choosing these methods over receiving advance payments directly. One accountant also noted that escrow is not a common arrangement, pointing out that a company can earn at least interest simply by receiving the money directly and putting it to work, so there is no reason to deposit it with a third party.

Samsung Electronics is in a different situation. Its contract liabilities, which had stayed within a set range for several years, rose by about KRW 8.5 trillion quarter on quarter in the second quarter of this year to KRW 23.6 trillion as it signed long term agreements with large customers. The company said advance payments would grow further as additional contracts were signed. In the third quarter, however, contract liabilities related to advance payments rose by only about KRW 550 billion. The industry believes Samsung may have reduced the amount of upfront payments in the additional contracts in exchange for raising the price cap on semiconductors. If this interpretation is correct, it does not match the company's description of "sufficiently binding LTAs." This is because lower advance payments make it easier for customers to walk away from contracts if the memory supply and demand balance reverses.

2026-11-26 06:17 北京时间 ｜ Source: @aleabitoreddit ｜ 原帖链接

$CRWV 的投行正在筹备一笔 45 亿美元的贷款出售，以其 $META 合同对应的 GPU 作抵押。
初步定价讨论在 7%–7.5%，需求已与发行规模相当。
看起来，尽管有"AI 信贷压力"的担忧，市场对与 hyperscaler 挂钩的融资仍有强劲胃口。
此前市场对 CoreWeave 困境债务（例如 2031 年债券收益率 18%）以及无法再融资存在条件反射式担忧。
但看起来它仍能通过与 hyperscaler 挂钩的建设继续增长。
TLDR：对有 hyperscaler 交易对手的 Neocloud 增长而言，投资级资本依然可得（利好 $IREN / $NBIS 等）。
更大的名字如 $SPCX 的 440 亿美元 GPU 融资更是超额认购。
AI 正在进入一个更看重资本选择性、而不是完全没有资本的阶段。（附两张图）

原文：$CRWV bankers are preparing a $4.5B loan sale, secured by GPUs for its $META contract.

Initial pricing discussions are at 7%-7.5% with demand already matching offering size.

It looks like appetite for hyperscaler-linked financing remains strong, despite the "AI credit stress" fears.

There were reflexive concerns about Coreweave distressed debt (eg. 2031 bond yields at 18%) and not being able to raise more capital.

But looks like they're still able to grow through hyperscaler-linked buildouts.

TLDR: Investment-grade capital remains available for Neocloud growth with hyperscaler counterparties. (good for $IREN / $NBIS / co).

And obviously larger names like $SPCX with their oversubscribed $44B GPU financing.

AI is moving to a phase more about capital selectivity rather than no capital at all.

存储：DRAM 价格温和下修
• 据领先的存储研究机构 @trendforce，DRAM 价格进入 2027 年可能趋缓。
我们的看法 ⬇️
• 2027 年 1Q/2Q/3Q/4Q 的 DRAM 合约价展望下修 → 现为环比 +6%/+3%/0%/0% → 此前为 +10%/+7%/+5%/+3%
• GPU 短缺导致中国 CSP 的服务器 DRAM 订单疲软（约占 DRAM 总需求的 1%，美国 CSP 可以消化）
• 消费级 DRAM 疲软 + 规格下调 + DDR4 承压
• 供需仍将良好：2026/27/28E 的供给缺口为 -5%/-5%/-4%，由 HBM 挤占晶圆产能驱动 → 若疲软持续，可能减产（以支撑 LTA 底价）→ DRAM 供需平衡好于 NAND
影响
• 由于下修幅度有限，仍对 Micron（MU）、SK Hynix（SKHY）持建设性看法
• 对 DDR4 厂商南亚科（2408 TT）和华邦（2344 TT）不利
#DRAM #Memory #MU #SKHY #DDR4（引用 TrendForce：中国 CSP 削减 2027 年存储订单，NAND Flash 将进入价格下行周期）

原文：Memory: Modest DRAM Pricing Downward Revision

•According to @trendforce , the leading memory research firm, DRAM price is likely to moderate into 2027 (link below) 
https://t.co/m3SOzqLQpq

For us, ⬇️

•DRAM contract price outlook revised down for 1Q/2Q/3Q/4Q27 → Now +6%/+3%/0%/0% QoQ → vs. prior +10%/+7%/+5%/+3%
•GPU shortage led to Chinese CSP server DRAM order softness (~1% of total DRAM demand, absorbable by US CSPs)
•Consumer DRAM soft + spec downgrades + pressure on DDR4
•Supply demand to remain fine: Sufficiency gap of -5%/-5%/-4% in 2026/27/28E driven by HBM crowding out wafer capacity  → Potential production cuts if weakness persists (to support LTA floor) → DRAM S/D balance better than NAND

Implications
•Still Constructive on Micron (MU), SK Hynix (SKHY) due to limited downward revision
•Unfavorable for DDR4 Nanya (2408 TT) and Winbond (2344 TT)

大家对华为即将在 ISSCC 2027 上发表的 4096-DQ 定制 HBM（采用 5 层 W2W Hybrid Bonding）感到兴奋。这解释了为什么即将推出的 Ascend 芯片会拥有比 Nvidia 更快的 HBM。
但 3D 堆叠千安级供电（3D-Stacked Kilo-Amp Power delivery）和面向 3D 友好 SerDes 的环形 PLL（Ring-Based PLL）对 Atlas 系统同样关键。你需要创新设计来为 3D 堆叠的存储/逻辑晶圆供电；也需要创新来支持 Atlas-960 上 8T NPO 互连所需的每光通道 200G。（附图）

原文：Ppl are excited about HW's upcoming presentation @ ISSCC 2027 on 4096-DQ custom HBM w/ 5-layer W2W Hybrid Bonding. That explains why upcoming Ascend chips will have faster HBM than Nvidia.

But the 3D-Stacked Kilo-Amp Power delivery & the Ring-Based PLL for 3D-Friendly serdes are also critical for the Atlas system. You need innovative design to provide power for 3D stacked memory/logic wafers. You also need innovation to support the 200G per optical lane needed for the 8T NPO interconnect that will be on Atlas-960.

2026-11-26 11:54 北京时间 ｜ Source: @semivision_tw ｜ 原帖链接

超构表面（metasurface）和超构透镜（metalens）从根本上改写了这一范式。它们不用厚透镜元件，而是在超薄薄膜上图案化一层亚波长结构——纳米柱或纳米鳍的密集阵列。每个纳米结构就像一个"像素化的相位延迟元件"，通过几何尺寸、形状和折射率精确调节透射光的相位、振幅和偏振。一旦在晶圆上图案化，一片 12 英寸晶圆可容纳数千乃至数万个超构透镜——我们的幻灯片展示了一片晶圆上的 5,000 个超构透镜，每个直径约 1 毫米，放大 20,000 倍可见密集的纳米柱阵列。（附图）

原文：Metasurfaces and metalenses fundamentally rewrite this paradigm. Instead of a thick lens element, they pattern a layer of subwavelength structures—dense arrays of nanopillars or nanofins—on an ultra-thin film. Each nanostructure functions like a “pixelated phase-delay element,” precisely tuning the phase, amplitude, and polarization of transmitted light through geometric dimensions, shape, and refractive index. Once patterned on a wafer, a single 12-inch wafer can contain thousands or even tens of thousands of metalenses—our slide illustrates 5,000 metalenses on one wafer, each roughly 1 mm in diameter, with a 20,000× magnification revealing the dense nanopillar array.

https://t.co/vy3XT6Tv3w

2026-11-26 07:03 北京时间 ｜ Source: @jasonschips ｜ 原帖链接

🔔 The Daily Semi-Cap — 2026 年 11 月 25 日
📊 SMH：$422.46（较前一交易日收盘 -1.01%）
• 花旗和渣打正在收紧对东南亚数据中心客户的审查，与区域性银行一道要求披露 AI 芯片最终用户及出口管制合规情况，Neocloud 受到特别审视。风险正从 GPU 获取转移到融资可得性：一个已通电的园区仍需要一个可融资、合法可服务的客户。
• Amazon 首次提交的 Trainium3 UltraServer 结果在 AgentX 的 agentic 推理基准中，每美元性能比 GB300 NVL72 高 2.28 倍。该工作负载侧重长上下文、前缀复用和 sub-agent 突发。这是特定工作负载的结果，并非对 GPU 的普遍替代，但加强了定制芯片在生产推理中的经济性论据。
• 随着 scale-up 部署进入量产，三季度 AI 后端以太网交换机销售额增长超过一倍。1.6Tbps 端口出货在首个量产季度即超过 100 万。Celestica 和 Nvidia 并列销售额第一；Arista 份额提升 2 个百分点，Cisco 增长最快。网络需求正从 scale-out 架构向外扩展。
• Samsung 新披露的 48GB HBM4E 设计达到每 pin 17Gbps，此前披露的最高为 16Gbps，提升 6.25%。2,048-bit 接口意味着约 4.35TB/s 的理论带宽。其 2 月的技术报告抬高了下一代存储的竞争门槛；但这并不代表已通过客户认证或具备量产出货条件。
• SpaceX 为其 Cochise County 超算项目规划了超过 500 亿美元投资，其中 36 亿美元用于电网升级。设计明确不建燃气电厂、不使用工业用水，采用闭环水系统的干式冷却。这些仍是开发承诺，但显示出电力资金和冷却架构正日益决定数据中心的执行。
• FCC 有条件批准了 SpaceX 最多 100 万颗轨道数据中心卫星的 Starmind 星座。卫星主要通过激光链路通信，以无线电为备份。获批并不意味着算力即将到位：发射经济性、处理器散热和融资仍是障碍，600 公里以上的碎片处置计划仍需进一步批准。

原文：🔔 The Daily Semi-Cap — November 25, 2026

📊 SMH: $422.46 (-1.01% vs prior trading close)

• Citigroup and Standard Chartered are tightening reviews of Southeast Asian datacenter clients, joining regional lenders in demanding disclosure of AI-chip end users and export-control compliance. Neoclouds face particular scrutiny. The risk is migrating from GPU access into financing availability: a powered campus still needs a bankable, legally serviceable customer.

• Amazon’s first Trainium3 UltraServer submission posted a 2.28x performance-per-dollar advantage over GB300 NVL72 in AgentX’s agentic-inference benchmark. The workload stresses long context, prefix reuse and sub-agent bursts. This is a workload-specific result, not universal GPU displacement, but strengthens the economic case for custom silicon in production inference.

• AI back-end Ethernet switch sales more than doubled in Q3 as scale-up deployments entered volume production. 1.6Tbps port shipments exceeded one million in their first volume quarter. Celestica and Nvidia shared the sales lead; Arista gained two share points and Cisco gained fastest. Networking demand is broadening beyond scale-out fabrics.

• Samsung’s newly disclosed 48GB HBM4E design reaches 17Gbps per pin, versus its previously disclosed 16Gbps maximum—a 6.25% increase. The 2,048-bit interface implies roughly 4.35TB/s of theoretical bandwidth. Its February technical presentation raises the competitive bar for next-generation memory; it does not establish customer qualification or volume-shipment readiness.

• SpaceX outlined more than $50B of investment for its Cochise County supercompute project, including $3.6B for grid upgrades. The design specifies no gas plant, no industrial water use and dry cooling with a closed-loop water system. These remain development commitments, but show how power funding and cooling architecture increasingly shape datacenter execution.

• The FCC conditionally authorized SpaceX’s Starmind constellation of up to one million orbital datacenter satellites. Satellites would primarily communicate through laser links, with radio backup. Approval does not mean imminent compute capacity: launch economics, processor cooling and financing remain hurdles, and debris plans above 600 kilometers still require further approval.

천하의 병신이 VC 출신인데 지들만 모름ㅋ

모가지에 깁스 처한 닭대가리들😉

★@levie（保留 1 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 1 条）

2026-11-26 14:20 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@levie ｜ 原帖链接

编程让你得以窥见大量知识工作未来的样子。
对大多数工作来说，第一步是：我们会从手动完成大部分研究、打字和价值创造（即便是用数字工具），很快转向协调 agent 正在做的工作。
接下来，当这一步本身变得多余、或者拖慢了 agent 的能力发挥时，我们会给 agent 设定要优化的目标，让它们自行去解决越来越大的问题。
乍看之下，这种影响似乎只会局限在编程领域，但实际上，由于能带来巨大的生产率提升，很大比例的知识工作最终可能会收敛到这种形态。Agent 会使用电脑、利用我们现有的工具或编写新工具，并即时写代码，以解决更大范围的知识工作。
所以，即使某项任务今天看起来并不直接是"编程"，它最终也可能变成与编程相邻的工作。制造、建筑、生命科学、材料科学、网络安全，等等。
最终一个很大的障碍是：这类工作中有很多仍然不可验证，所以人类会比在编程中更多地参与其中（in the loop）。但随着模型能力提升，人类能交出去的那部分会越来越大。
简而言之，关注编程，你就能感受到工作的走向。

原文：Coding gives you a window into what the future will look like for a large amount of knowledge work.

The first step for most jobs is that we go from doing most of the research, typing, and value creation manually (even if it’s using digital tools) to soon coordinating the work that agents are doing.

Next, as this step itself becomes redundant or slows down what agents are capable of, we will be giving agents goals to optimize against and they’ll go off on and increasingly solve larger problems for us.

This may at first blush look like something whose impact gets stuck with coding, but actually a large percentage of knowledge work may eventually just converge with this form factor due to the massive productivity gains that can be generated. Agents will use computers, leverage our existing tools or write new ones, and write code on the fly to solve larger swaths of knowledge work.

So even if a task today doesn’t seem to be “coding” directly, the task may eventually fold into being coding adjacent. Manufacturing, architecture, life sciences, material sciences, cyber, and on and on.

One big hurdle in the way ultimately will be that much of this work remains non-verifiable, so humans will be even more in the loop than they are in coding. But the part that they can hand off will just get bigger and bigger over time as the models are more capable.

So in short, pay attention to coding and you’ll get a sense for where work is going.引用原帖（@?）：We're in an insane moment right now, and it's because of AI. There's a weird duality because AI coding tools are also extraordinarily helpful to our business. But coding gives me a front-row seat to see how fast things are progressing. The growth in capabilities is very fast. 链接

2026-11-26 14:45 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@tszzl ｜ 原帖链接

注意，理论物理的结果不像数学结果。这类论文的部分内容可以用"形式化验证"来检查，但很多是物理学家式的论证，而它是否真的描述了自然，不是仅凭一篇论文就能证明的。目前它必须由其他专家来逐条推敲，所以我不想把期望抬得太高。
理论物理构建在某些情形下成立的数学框架，并试图让它们越来越贴近现实，然后去预测或解释之前的理论无法解释的物理现象。这篇是真空中（尚无物质）的量子理论，在大尺度上能复原黑洞熵公式和爱因斯坦方程等已知结果，但据我理解，这些只是此类理论必须通过的最低限度检验。
对只在极高能量下才出现的量子引力修正做直接实验，以现有技术不可能——你只能寄希望于该理论能解释例如宇宙微波背景中的某些现象。
Ven 在这里给伯克利的一个小组做了报告，当然也有大量质疑。

原文：note that results in theoretical physics aren't like results in math. parts of a paper like this can be checked with 'formal verification', but a lot of it is physicist argument, and whether it actually describes nature isn't something you can prove in a paper alone. for now, it has to be picked apart by other experts, so i don't want to raise hopes too much

theoretical physics constructs mathematical frameworks that work in some settings and tries to make them increasingly realistic, and then try to predict or explain some physical phenomena that previous ideas couldn't. this one is a quantum theory in a vaccum (no matter yet) that recovers known results like the black hole entropy formula and the einstein equations at large scales, but as i understand it these are the minimum tests such a theory would have to pass

direct experiments for quantum gravity corrections that only show up at incredibly high energies are not possible with current technology - you would have to hope the theory explains something in the cosmic microwave background for example

ven gives a talk here to a berkeley group, and there is certainly plenty of pushback
https://t.co/ciBRGUOGxC所回复原帖（@tszzl）：ven chandrasekaran’s proposal for a quantum theory of vacuum gravity in 4d

https://t.co/BmSlfGyj7p 链接

@karpathy（保留 2 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 2 条）

2026-11-26 10:31 北京时间 ｜ Source: @karpathy ｜ 原帖链接

我让 Claude 用美国的真实数据填充一张《文明》风格的游戏地图。Opus 5.5 下载并解析了大量公开数据，7 个小时、4.5 万行代码之后……它跑起来了 :)
- 31 个真实数据源，包括 Census、BEA、BLS、EIA、NOAA、USGS、DoD、NPS、FAA、FEMA、CDC……
- 真实的海拔、土地覆盖、GDP、就业、发电厂、矿山、港口、机场、军事基地、公园、大学、货运流、灾害；全部统一到一个 25 公里的六边形网格上。
- 数据被转换成《文明》的语言，例如 Gold 产出就是 GDP。城市的区域就是其真实的产业专长：San Jose 得到 Campus，Detroit 是"汽车城"。市民就是真实劳动力：San Jose 每 4 名劳动者中就有 1 名工程师、程序员或科学家，而全国是 14 分之 1。奇观按公开规则选定，例如国家公园按游客数、大学按研发支出等等。
- 19 种视图（收入、学历、信仰、灾害……）、524 个都市区的 City View、九位顾问外加一个 Explorer。Explorer 可以把 3,100 多个县的任意一列变成表格和散点图，并与地图联动。
- Claude 决定跳过几个州和领地，抱歉
- 使用 Claude Code 桌面应用构建，Opus 5.5（medium）并打开 ultracode。
已部署上线，"?"里也有源码链接。我也不太清楚我想拿它干嘛，但浏览和把玩非常有趣。也许可以扩展或做成游戏。不过我每周的 Claude 额度只剩一半了，所以得有别人来接手 :D。Claude 还做了个预告片（附视频）。

原文：I asked Claude to populate a Civilization-style game map with real data from the US. Opus 5.5 downloaded and parse a ton of public data and 7 hours and 45K lines of code later... it works :)

- 31 real data sources including Census, BEA, BLS, EIA, NOAA, USGS, DoD, NPS, FAA, FEMA, CDC...
- Real elevation, land cover, GDP, jobs, power plants, mines, ports, airports, bases, parks, universities, freight flows, disasters; all reconciled onto one 25 km hex grid.
- The data is converted into Civilization language, e.g. Gold yield is GDP. A city's districts are its real industry specializations: San Jose gets a Campus, Detroit is "Motor city". Its citizens are its real workforce: 1 in 4 San Jose workers are engineers, coders or scientists, against 1 in 14 nationally. Wonders are chosen by published rules, such as national parks by visitor numbers and universities by R&D spending, etc etc.
- 19 lenses (income, degrees, faith, disasters…), a City View for 524 metros, and nine advisors plus an Explorer. The Explorer turns any column for any of 3,100+ counties into a table and scatter plot, linked to the map.
- Claude decided to skip a few states and territories sry
- Built using Claude Code desktop app, Opus 5.5 on medium and turning on ultracode.

It's deployed here: https://t.co/QMhmafXMj5 and the "?" has the source link too. I don't exactly know where I was going with this but it's really fun to browse and play with. Possibly it can be expanded or built into a game. I only have half of my weekly Claude quote left though so someone else will have to take over :D. Claude also made a trailer:（video：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

@yukangchen_（保留 3 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 5 条）

2026-11-26 14:15 北京时间 ｜ Source: @yukangchen_ ｜ 原帖链接

"她加了几朵向日葵"——如果你的视频模型记得画布上已经有什么，这就是一个完全够用的 prompt。
我们的画家在山间湖畔支起画架，画下湖对岸的雪峰。几个镜头后，她站在向日葵田里，把黄色花瓣刷进前景。她在湖边画的群山依然在画上。最后一个镜头里，这幅画挂在她的沙发上方。每个地方的一点点都随她回了家。
另一个 demo 里，我们跟着一位装机者从工作台走到他新电脑上玩的第一款游戏。他组装一块 GPU 卡，装进白色机箱，并在水冷回路里灌满亮绿色冷却液。故事进行到第五分钟，他终于坐下来玩时，那仍然是他的电脑——透过机箱侧板还能看到绿色水管。
Agentic LongLive 在实时运行的同时解决了长视频生成中的记忆问题。你逐个镜头地引导故事。每个镜头播放时，一个 memory agent 会更新你引入的人物、物体和地点的视觉参考。在我们较长的故事里，14 分钟后你仍能认出开场镜头里的人物。
同样的思路也有助于机器人仿真。项目页上的一个 demo 里，机器人从桌上的风扇转身去处理一个包裹，半分钟后回头，风扇依然在那里，就在橙色椅子旁边。
在项目页上，把鼠标悬停在故事上可以看到视频播放时 agent 记住了什么 👇（附项目页、论文、代码链接与视频）

原文：“She adds a few sunflowers” is a perfectly good prompt if your video model remembers what’s already on the canvas.

Our painter sets up her easel by a mountain lake and paints the snowy peaks across the water. A few shots later, she’s standing in a sunflower field, brushing yellow petals into the foreground. The mountains she painted by the lake are still there. In the last shot, the painting hangs above her sofa. A little of each place has come home with her.

In the other demo, we follow a builder from his workbench to the first game he plays on his new PC. He assembles a GPU card, installs it in a white case, and fills the cooling loop with bright green coolant. Five minutes into the story, when he finally sits down to play, it’s still his PC. You can see the green tubing through the side of the case.

Agentic LongLive solves the memory issue in long video generation while running in real time. You steer the story one shot at a time. While each shot plays, a memory agent updates visual references for the people, objects, and places you’ve introduced. In our longer stories, you can still recognize the person from the opening shot 14 minutes later.

The same idea helps in robotics simulation. In one demo on the project page, a robot turns away from a desk fan to handle a package. Half a minute later, it looks back. The fan is still there, right beside the orange chair.

On the project page, hover over a story to see what the agent remembers as the video plays 👇

2026-11-26 18:06 北京时间 ｜ Source: @teortaxesTex ｜ 原帖链接

＞ 归根结底，在我看来这是规模问题：小模型不具备它，与 RL 无关
我的先验：任何特定能力都可以用线性增长的数据或指数增长的算力×参数买到。通用网络语料几乎包含一切，但形态零散，把其中内容关联起来的成本高得令人望而却步。如果数据足够好，小模型完全可以做 AI4S。Inkling 展示了这条路。

原文：> Ultimately, in my opinion, it's a scaling issue: small models lack it, regardless of RL
my prior: any particular capability can be bought with linearly more data OR exponentially more compute*params. The general web corpus has ≈everything, but in an inchoate form, and correlating its contents is prohibitively expensive.
Small models can easily do AI4S… if the data is that good. Inkling demonstrates the way.引用原帖（@4staying）：@teortaxesTex I work in biology/AI4S. V4P and K3 are the only open-weight models with the right "taste" for my field. Sub-1T models (DS f 0713-4.1, GLM, Luna?) have always been unusable. Ultimately, in my opinion, it's a scaling issue: small models lack it, regardless of RL. They need a PRO. 链接

2026-11-26 18:40 北京时间 ｜ Source: @teortaxesTex ｜ 原帖链接

我们怎么会知道？其实这是个有趣的门槛。我发现给模型提供定制模块/工具通常适得其反，它们能在运行时构建更好的专用工具，而不必浪费 token 去读之前陌生的上下文。它们在自己的流形里很舒服。一门预训练中不存在的新语言，必须跨过很高的门槛。（引用："奇怪大厂还没有 vibecode 出一门新语言"）

原文：How would we know?
Actually this is an interesting threshold. I found that providing models with bespoke modules/tools is usually counterproductive, they can construct better purpose-specific ones at runtime, without wasting tokens on reading prior alien context. They are comfortable in their own manifold.
A new language, absent from pretraining, has to clear a HIGH bar.引用原帖（@?）：surprised none of the big labs vibecoded a new language yet 链接

➤ 文字渲染涵盖长短文本、2D 版式、非词汇字符串以及变形表面上的文字。
➤ UI/UX & Motion Design 涵盖动态排版、跨设备 UI 动效演示和抽象动态图形。
➤ Dialogue & Lip Sync 涵盖单人/多人对白，以及画外音与画内说话。
➤ Human Anatomy 涵盖步态与移动、精细动作、面部表情、杂技动作、抓取物体和人际互动。（附图）

原文：In Image to Video, MAGI-2 stands out on UI motion graphics, social clips, and marketing spots, with #1 audio timing, text rendering, and multi-shot consistency

From a starting image on AA-Video-I2V v2.1, MAGI-2 ranks top 3 in every capability and leads the board in Audio Synchronization, Spatio-Temporal Consistency, Text Rendering, and Multi-Scene & Narrative, as well as UI/UX & Motion Design, Social Media & Creator Content, Marketing & Advertising, and Architecture & Real Estate.

Its main remaining headroom against Dreamina Seedance 2.5 is in spoken dialogue (Dialogue & Lip Sync), human movement and expression (Human Anatomy), complex camera moves (Camera Control), and cinematic shots (Live-Action Film).

➤ Audio Synchronization covers diegetic sound effects, background music and score, and on- and off-screen sound.

➤ Spatio-Temporal Consistency covers spatial coherence, time lapses, occlusion and re-entry, and single-shot stories.

➤ Text Rendering covers short and long text, 2D layout, non-lexical strings, and text on deforming surfaces.

2026-11-26 11:34 北京时间 ｜ Source: @JosephJacks_ ｜ 原帖链接

血清素究竟如何"编程"神经元微管？
极性的血清素（cLogP 0.57）无法被动穿过皮层锥体细胞膜，而这些神经元缺少 SERT——所以细胞外的 5-HT 停留在表面，在 5-HT2A/4/7（稳定作用）与 5-HT1A（去稳定作用）之间进行推拉。
亲脂性的促神经可塑性分子如 DMT、psilocin 和 5-MeO-DMT（cLogP 2.33）可直接穿过细胞膜，激活细胞内的 5-HT2A 受体池（Vargas & Olson, Science 2023），电镜显示该受体池与 MAP1A 一起直接共定位于树突微管上（Cornea-Hébert et al., 2002）。
从那里开始，MAP2 充当胞体-树突的"编译器"。下游的 Ca2+ 和 cAMP 触发预先停靠在 MAP2 N 端 AKAP 基序上的 CaMKII、PKC、MARK 和 PKA-RII，使三个 KXGS 丝氨酸（S1682、S1713、S1745）磷酸化。
每个 PO4(2-) 基团都增加负电荷，与微管蛋白酸性的 E-hook 产生静电排斥，把 MAP2 从微管"轨道"上剥离并推向树突棘的 F-actin——同时胞质 5-HT 驱动 TG2 对 Rac1 的血清素化修饰，使 EB3 +TIPs 能把正在生长的微管引导进正在重塑的树突棘。
清除 MAP2 解除了它对 Kinesin-1（KIF5）约 15 倍的空间位阻，并恢复 KIF17 对 GluN2B NMDA 受体的运输。在小鼠前额叶皮层的体内实验中，全身给予 psilocybin 可以可测量地改写微管蛋白编码本身：Lys40 乙酰化 α-微管蛋白 -30.5%，酪氨酸化 α-微管蛋白 +18.9%（Maltby et al., 2026）。
以上都是已确立的生物学——正好位于"晶格编码层"的上游：约 20nm 的 CaMKII 十二聚体在周期性的 MAP2 交联桥之间停靠到"6 围 1"的微管蛋白二聚体上（每字节 64 到 5,281 种状态）。
突触接收血清素；胞体-树突的 MAP 把它"编译"到微管晶格上。（附图）

原文：How does serotonin actually program neuronal microtubules ?

Polar serotonin (cLogP 0.57) cannot passively cross cortical pyramidal cell membranes, and those neurons lack SERT — so extracellular 5-HT stays at the surface, running a push-pull between 5-HT2A/4/7 (stabilizing) and 5-HT1A (destabilizing).

Lipophilic psychoplastogens like DMT, psilocin and 5-MeO-DMT (cLogP 2.33) cross straight through the membrane to activate an intracellular pool of 5-HT2A receptors (Vargas & Olson, Science 2023) that electron microscopy shows colocalizes with MAP1A directly on dendritic microtubules (Cornea-Hébert et al., 2002).

From there, MAP2 acts as the somatodendritic compiler. Downstream Ca2+ and cAMP trigger CaMKII, PKC, MARK and PKA-RII pre-docked directly onto MAP2's N-terminal AKAP motif to phosphorylate three KXGS serines (S1682, S1713, S1745).

Each PO4(2-) group adds negative charge that electrostatically repels tubulin's acidic E-hooks, peeling MAP2 off the microtubule rail toward spine F-actin — while cytosolic 5-HT drives TG2 serotonylation of Rac1 so EB3 +TIPs can steer growing microtubules into remodeling dendritic spines.

Clearing MAP2 lifts its ~15x steric block on Kinesin-1 (KIF5) and restores KIF17 delivery of GluN2B NMDA receptors. In vivo in mouse prefrontal cortex, systemic psilocybin measurably rewrites the tubulin code itself:

-30.5% Lys40 acetylated alpha-tubulin and +18.9% tyrosinated alpha-tubulin (Maltby et al., 2026).

That much is established biology — sitting right upstream of the lattice-code layer, where ~20 nm CaMKII dodecamers dock onto 6-around-1 tubulin dimers (64 to 5,281 states per byte) between periodic MAP2 cross-bridges.

Synapses receive serotonin; somatodendritic MAPs compile it onto the microtubule lattice.

2026-11-26 11:37 北京时间 ｜ Source: @JosephJacks_ ｜ 原帖链接

这张示意图背后的原始论文和来源：

https://t.co/iCFfTE0qJW所回复原帖（@JosephJacks_）：How does serotonin actually program neuronal microtubules ?

Polar serotonin (cLogP 0.57) cannot passively cross cortical pyramidal cell membranes, and those neurons lack SERT — so extracellular 5-HT stays at the surface, running a push-pull between 5-HT2A/4/7 (stabilizing) and 5-HT1A (destabilizing).

Lipophilic psychoplastogens like DMT, psilocin and 5-MeO-DMT (cLogP 2.33) cross straight through… 链接

【无关/无bio】

@Omercheema（保留 2 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 2 条）

2026-11-26 16:42 北京时间 ｜ Source: @Omercheema ｜ 原帖链接

关于 AI 是否会颠覆 EDA 的问题经常被提起。
EDA 市场是一个持久的双寡头（Synopsys 和 Cadence），Siemens EDA 是明确的第三名。客户转换成本极高。设计流程、验证环境、IP 库、晶圆厂 PDK 以及多年的流片计划都与这些平台紧密耦合。可靠性、可复现性和 sign-off 要求进一步巩固了现状。实际上，领先 EDA 厂商的地位看起来至少和 ASML 在 EUV 光刻上的地位一样难以撼动。两者都受益于深厚的技术护城河、漫长的认证周期，以及大客户不愿在关键路径工具上冒险。
专注于 AI 辅助生产力的新兴创业公司——RTL 生成、验证加速、设计探索或工作流自动化的单点工具——正在引起真正的兴趣。这些产品可以在设计流程的狭窄环节带来可衡量的效率提升。然而，集成负担、数据要求以及对生产级 sign-off 的需求，构成了成为主流程的高门槛。这些公司中最强者最可能的出路，是被三大 EDA 厂商之一收购，再由后者把能力嵌入现有平台和分销渠道。
简而言之，AI 更多是在 EDA 栈内部提升生产力，而不是改写行业竞争结构。双寡头格局依然完好。

原文：The question of AI-driven disruption in EDA is coming up regularly.

The EDA market is a durable duopoly (Synopsys and Cadence) with Siemens EDA as a clear third. Customer switching costs are exceptionally high. Design flows, verification environments, IP libraries, foundry PDKs, and multi-year tape-out schedules are tightly coupled to these platforms. Reliability, reproducibility, and sign-off requirements further entrench the status quo. In practical terms, the position of the leading EDA vendors appears at least as defensible as ASML’s position in EUV lithography. Both benefit from deep technical moats, long qualification cycles, and limited willingness by large customers to take risk on critical path tools.

Emerging startups focused on AI-assisted productivity—point tools for RTL generation, verification acceleration, design exploration, or workflow automation—are generating genuine interest. These offerings can deliver measurable efficiency gains in narrow slices of the design process. However, the integration burden, data requirements, and need for production-grade sign-off create high barriers to becoming a primary flow. The most plausible path for the strongest of these companies is acquisition by one of the top three EDA vendors, which can then embed the capability into an existing platform and distribution channel.

In short, AI is improving productivity inside the EDA stack more than it is rewriting the competitive structure of the industry. The duopoly remains intact.

2026-11-26 16:49 北京时间 ｜ Source: @Omercheema ｜ 原帖链接

[k1｜HBM/DRAM/NAND / SKHY/SKhynix] 2026-11-26 15:30 北京时间 ｜ Source: @MelvinInvests（名单外） ｜ 原帖链接

据韩国媒体报道，华盛顿刚刚把 Samsung 和 SK Hynix 直接放进了瞄准镜。
USTR 新的 Section 301 报告点名两家对存储市场的合计控制力：全球 DRAM 超过 70%、NAND 51%。
报告矛头指向韩国的税收优惠、廉价融资、电力和用水支持，以及其到 2030 年把芯片出口翻倍至 2,000 亿美元的目标。报告还指出，韩国承诺在二十年内对该产业投入至少 5,300 亿美元。
好消息是 DRAM、NAND 和 HBM 目前豁免于拟议的 25% 关税，但它们仍处于单独的 Section 232 框架之下。
我认为这是筹码。美国希望在本土建更多存储晶圆厂，这就是让 Samsung 和 SK Hynix 坐上谈判桌的方式。
如果这演变为对韩国存储的实质压力，$MU 是显而易见的赢家。（此为名单外作者说法，未经本摘要核实）

原文：According to Korean media, Washington just put Samsung and SK Hynix directly in the crosshairs.

The USTR's new Section 301 report calls out their combined control of the memory market, with over 70% of global DRAM and 51% of NAND.

The report goes after Korea's tax breaks, cheap financing, power and water support, and its goal of doubling chip exports to $200 billion by 2030. It also notes Korea is committed to spend at least $530 billion on the industry over two decades.

The good news is DRAM, NAND, and HBM are EXEMPT from the proposed 25% tariffs for now. They still sit under a separate Section 232 framework though.

I think this is LEVERAGE. The United States wants more memory fabs built here, and this is how you get Samsung and SK Hynix to the table.

$MU is the obvious winner if this turns into real pressure on Korean memory.

[k4｜HBM/DRAM/NAND / SKHY/SKhynix / MU/Micron] 2026-11-26 19:30 北京时间 ｜ Source: @MelvinInvests（名单外） ｜ 原帖链接

SK Hynix 刚刚公布了存储领域下一次重大转变的计划。
公司的首款 3D DRAM 芯片目标为 100 层，量产时间约在 2030–2031 年。
与 HBM 堆叠成品芯片不同，3D DRAM 是在每颗芯片内部垂直堆叠存储单元——基本上就是十年前 2D 微缩变难后 NAND 所做的事。
其工程师表示，大约需要 75 层才能追平今天的 DRAM 密度，所以 100 层是开始见效的位置。
他们已经做出了表现与普通 DRAM 一致的测试单元。
这还很遥远，但谁先做好 3D DRAM，谁就拥有存储的下一个十年。目前这场竞赛在 SK Hynix、Samsung 和 $MU 之间。（此为名单外作者说法，未经本摘要核实）

原文：SK Hynix just laid out its plan for the next big shift in memory.

The company is targeting 100 layers for its first 3D DRAM chip, with mass production around 2030 to 2031.

Instead of stacking finished chips like HBM, 3D DRAM stacks the memory cells vertically INSIDE each chip. Basically what NAND did a decade ago, once 2D scaling got harder.

Their engineers say it takes about 75 layers just to match today's DRAM density, so 100 layers is where it starts to pay off.

They have already built test cells that behave like normal DRAM.

This is a long way out, but whoever gets 3D DRAM right first owns the next decade of memory. Right now that race is between SK Hynix, Samsung, and $MU.

$MU

[k6｜SKHY/SKhynix / HBM/DRAM/NAND] 2026-11-26 18:09 北京时间 ｜ Source: @J2Wick（名单外） ｜ 原帖链接

Samsung 和 SK hynix 的股价已经计入了 36–46% 的存储价格下跌
这是对日益悲观的存储展望的一个有趣反证。
三星证券在 11 月 26 日的报告中估计，当前估值已经隐含的存储价格跌幅为：
• Samsung Electronics：46%
• $SKHY SK hynix：36%
该分析基于 2010 年以来历史 trailing P/B 与 forward ROE 的关系。
这些不是存储价格预测，而是根据该券商模型、当前股票估值所隐含的恶化程度。
在一系列目标价下调和 2027 年可能供过于求的警告之后，我认为还有一个值得问的问题：下一轮存储下行有多少已经被计入价格？
这不一定意味着已经见底。但基本面恶化与预期恶化之间的区别很重要。
来源：三星证券 Tech Talk，2026 年 11 月 26 日，第 10 页。（此为名单外作者说法，未经本摘要核实）

原文：SAMSUNG AND SK HYNIX ARE ALREADY PRICING IN A 36–46% MEMORY PRICE DECLINE

An interesting counterpoint to the increasingly bearish memory outlook.

In its November 26 report, Samsung Securities estimates that current valuations already imply memory price declines of:

• Samsung Electronics: 46%
• $SKHY SK hynix: 36%

The analysis is based on the historical relationship between trailing P/B and forward ROE, using data going back to 2010.

These are not forecasts of memory prices. They represent the degree of deterioration implied by current equity valuations, according to the brokerage's model.

After a series of target price cuts and warnings about potential oversupply in 2027, I think there's another question worth asking.

How much of the next memory downturn is already priced in?

That doesn't necessarily mean we've reached a bottom. But the distinction between deteriorating fundamentals and deteriorating expectations matters.

Source: Samsung Securities, Tech Talk, Nov. 26, 2026, p. 10.

（中文原文）首爾經濟 11/24 的標題，直接寫高盛把三星電子（005930）、SK海力士（000660）目標價下修最多約 43%。後面幾家韓媒也跟著寫，市場聽到就想：高盛看空記憶體了。

[k8｜MU/Micron / SKHY/SKhynix / HBM/DRAM/NAND] 2026-11-26 11:26 北京时间 ｜ Source: @carrioresearch（名单外） ｜ 原帖链接

🚨 在 NAND 领域，中国已经不只是在追赶。
日经刚刚报道，Samsung 和 $SKHY 都已与中国的长江存储（$YMTC）签署交叉授权协议，以使用其 Xtacking 混合键合技术的部分内容。
全球前两大存储巨头，现在要向一家成立仅 10 年的中国晶圆厂授权核心架构。
为什么会这样，市场为何应当关心：
1. 400 层以上的专利墙：超过 400 层后，传统 NAND 会遇到应力极限，需要混合键合。长江存储很早就申请并筑起庞大的专利墙（据 KnowMade，混合键合专利 119 项，Samsung 83 项，SK hynix 仅 11 项）。
2. 韩国对手选择授权，$MU 则遭禁令：Samsung 和 SK hynix 清除了法律风险，而慕尼黑一家法院 9 月裁定美光侵犯长江存储两项 3D NAND 专利并颁发禁令（一审，上诉中）。
3. 规模已经很大：长江存储二季度 NAND 位元份额升至全球第三（约 14%），仅次于 Samsung（约 25%）和 SK hynix（约 22%）。其 IPO 文件显示仅 2026 年一季度收入就达 470 亿元人民币（约 70 亿美元）；TrendForce 刚指出，在消费需求疲弱和长江存储竞争下，NAND 现货价两周内下跌 20%。
4. 下一个目标：3D DRAM。随着武汉新厂爬坡，长江存储已在把晶圆键合技术从 NAND 延伸到 DRAM。
中国现在在存储领域拥有卡脖子级 IP。$MU $SKHY $SNDK $WDC $YMTC（此为名单外作者说法，未经本摘要核实）

原文：🚨 In NAND, China is already past catching up.

Nikkei just reported that both Samsung and $SKHY signed cross licensing deals with China’s $YMTC to use parts of its Xtacking hybrid bonding tech.

The world’s top two memory giants are now licensing core architecture from a 10 year old Chinese fab.

Why this happened and why the market should care:

1. The 400+ layer patent wall
Past 400 layers, traditional NAND hits stress limits and requires hybrid bonding. YMTC filed early and built a massive IP wall (119 hybrid bonding patents vs. 83 at Samsung and just 11 at SK hynix per KnowMade).

2. Korean rivals licensed. $MU got hit with injunctions.
While Samsung and SK hynix cleared legal risk, a Munich court ruled in September that Micron infringed two YMTC 3D NAND patents and granted injunctions (first instance, under appeal).

3. The scale is already massive
YMTC climbed to #3 globally in Q2 NAND bit share (~14%), trailing only Samsung (~25%) and SK hynix (~22%). Its IPO filing revealed RMB 47B (~$7B) in Q1 2026 revenue alone, and TrendForce just noted NAND spot prices slid 20% in two weeks amid weak consumer demand and YMTC competition.

4. Next target: 3D DRAM
YMTC is already extending its wafer bonding tech from NAND into DRAM as its new Wuhan fab ramps.

China now owns chokepoint IP in memory.

$MU $SKHY $SNDK $WDC $YMTC

存储买家正在提前数年锁定供应。美光年报显示了这些承诺有多长：截至 9 月 3 日，其 1,340 亿美元剩余履约义务（RPO）中，预计只有约四分之一会在未来 12 个月确认为收入。

原文：$MU $SKHY $SSNLF
Memory buyers are committing years ahead to secure supply. Micron’s annual filing shows how far out those commitments run: of its $134 billion in remaining performance obligations on Sept. 3, it expected to recognize only about a quarter as revenue over the next 12 months.

DIGITIMES reports that Micron, Samsung and SK hynix are each securing multiyear supply deals with customers. Micron disclosed binding purchase volumes under take-or-pay contracts. Most of its strategic agreements also set a price floor and a ceiling.

Customers get a firmer claim on future chips. For Micron, committed volumes and price floors make the downside easier to estimate, while the ceilings limit what it can charge if the shortage deepens.

[k12｜Kioxia / HBM/DRAM/NAND] 2026-11-26 19:07 北京时间 ｜ Source: @MojoTricks（名单外） ｜ 原帖链接

AI 的内存瓶颈可能有了新的绕行办法：SSD。
Kioxia 和 NVIDIA 在一套 GB200 NVL72 系统上测试了 144 块 SSD，把有效内存容量扩展到 1PB 以上。
NVIDIA 的 SCADA 软件让 GPU 在工作负载超出板载 HBM 容量时可以访问基于 SSD 的数据。
这对超大向量数据库、图分析和内存密集型 AI 应用意义重大。
虽然 SSD 无法在带宽或延迟上匹敌 HBM，但这种架构可以改善内存可扩展性并减少工作负载碎片化。
基于 SSD 的内存扩展会成为下一代 AI 基础设施的标准功能吗？$NVDA（此为名单外作者说法，未经本摘要核实）

原文：AI’s memory bottleneck may have a new workaround: SSDs.

Kioxia and NVIDIA tested 144 SSDs on a GB200 NVL72 system, expanding effective memory capacity beyond 1 petabyte.

NVIDIA’s SCADA software enables GPUs to access SSD-based data when workloads exceed onboard HBM capacity.

The implications are significant for massive vector databases, graph analytics, and memory-intensive AI applications.

While SSDs cannot match HBM bandwidth or latency, this architecture could improve memory scalability and reduce workload fragmentation.

Could SSD-based memory expansion become a standard feature of next-generation AI infrastructure?

$NVDA

③ 有报道称 Michael Burry 几乎平掉了空头仓位并建立了多头仓位。
④ 另外，美国知名投资者 Mark Minervini 也发帖称科技股超卖。
⑤ 昨天的 PCE 平减指数略强、支持美联储加息，但褐皮书整体偏鸽，缓和了加息压力。
以上是整体氛围改善的因素。考虑到 Advantest +4.57%、日本 Micronics +5.12%，我认为 Kioxia 约 +5% 是大盘带动，剩下约 7% 来自 Kioxia 自身利好。

⑦ 昨日收盘后公布的 IR：子公司超过 2 万亿日元的股息将转移至 Kioxia。确认了 2 万亿日元的大量现金，加上公司上次财报表示将积极回报股东，市场押注大规模回购。

キオクシアだけでなく地合いが良くなった材料はこのあたりかな🤔

10月末から株価が大きく下落して、当初の1兆円規模から約4,748億円規模へ縮小見込みとの日経の報道もあるけど、機械的な買い入れ有難い。

特にNVIDIAとのHBM実効容量拡張に向けたAI向け実証開始はかなり大きな発表だったと思う🤔
ただ、この発表は10時に公式発表でこの時点で既にストップ高水準まで上がっていたので、上げ材料というよりも下げを抑える材料だったかも🤔
